# Test durations the integration lane's CI shards split by, in seconds:
# `dir|package|Test|seconds` for each test of 1 s or more, and one
# `dir|package|*|seconds` line per package for the mean of the rest.
# scripts/lib-laneorder.sh (assign_shard) deals each package's tests out slowest
# first, to the shard with the least of that package's time so far.
#
# Read as weights, not predictions: a slow runner takes longer on every test.
# A test this file does not name weighs its package's `*` line, so letting the
# file go stale costs balance, never a test. Refresh it from a green CI run with
# `scripts/refresh-integration-test-timing.sh <run-id>`.
